	remove_modifier = $MOD$
	every_country = {
		remove_modifier = $MOD$
		every_scope_state = {
			remove_modifier = $MOD$
			every_scope_building = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
		}
		every_scope_building = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
		every_scope_character = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
		every_interest_group = {
			remove_modifier = $MOD$
			every_scope_character = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
		}
		every_political_movement = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
	}
	every_state = {
		remove_modifier = $MOD$
		every_scope_building = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
	}
	every_character = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
	every_character_in_exile_pool = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
	every_power_bloc = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
}
